interface Greeter {\n    fun greet(): String\n    fun loud(): String = greet().uppercase() + "!"\n}\nabstract class Base(val n: Int) {\n    abstract fun step(): Int\n    fun run(): Int = step() + n\n}\nclass Impl(n: Int, val m: Int) : Base(n), Greeter {\n    override fun step(): Int = m * 3\n    override fun greet(): String = "impl$m"\n}\nfun main() {\n    val i = Impl(2, 4)\n    println(i.run())\n    println(i.greet())\n    println(i.loud())\n    val b: Base = Impl(1, 5)\n    println(b.run())\n    println(b.step())\n    println(b.n)\n    val g: Greeter = Impl(0, 7)\n    println(g.loud())\n    println(i is Base)\n    println(i is Greeter)\n}\n	14\nimpl4\nIMPL4!\n16\n15\n1\nIMPL7!\ntrue\ntrue\n

class Outer(val name: String) {\n    var count = 0\n    private val secret = 7\n    fun bump() = ++count\n    inner class In(val k: Int) {\n        fun g() = "$name/$k/${secret + k}"\n        fun touch() { count += k; bump() }\n        fun me() = this@Outer.name + this@In.k\n        inner class Deep { fun d() = "$name-$k-$count" }\n    }\n    class Nest { fun h() = 7 }\n    fun make(k: Int) = In(k)\n}\nfun main() {\n    val o = Outer("o")\n    val i = o.In(3)\n    println(i.g())\n    i.touch(); println(o.count)\n    println(o.make(5).g())\n    println(i.me())\n    println(i.Deep().d())\n    println(Outer("x").In(1).g())\n    println(Outer.Nest().h())\n    println(Outer("p").In(2).Deep().d())\n}	o/3/10\n4\no/5/12\no3\no-3-4\nx/1/8\n7\np-2-0\n
open class Tree(val label: String) {\n    val items = mutableListOf<String>()\n    inner class Node(val v: Int = 1) {\n        val tag = "$label:$v"\n        init { items.add(tag) }\n        fun all() = items.map { "$it@$label" }\n        fun sum(xs: List<Int>) = xs.sumOf { it * v } + label.length\n        override fun toString() = "Node($tag)"\n    }\n    fun grow(n: Int) = (1..n).map { Node(it) }\n}\nclass Forest : Tree("f") { fun one() = Node() }\ninterface Shape { fun area(): Int }\nclass Grid(val w: Int) {\n    inner class Cell(val h: Int) : Shape { override fun area() = w * h }\n    fun cells() = listOf(Cell(2), Cell(3))\n}\nfun main() {\n    val t = Tree("t")\n    println(t.grow(3))\n    println(t.items)\n    val n = t.Node(4)\n    println(n.all())\n    println(n.sum(listOf(1, 2)))\n    println(Forest().one())\n    val g = Grid(5)\n    println(g.cells().map { it.area() })\n    val s: Shape = g.Cell(7)\n    println(s.area())\n    println(s is Grid.Cell)\n    val c: Grid.Cell = g.Cell(1)\n    println(c.h)\n}	[Node(t:1), Node(t:2), Node(t:3)]\n[t:1, t:2, t:3]\n[t:1@t, t:2@t, t:3@t, t:4@t]\n13\nNode(f:1)\n[10, 15]\n35\ntrue\n1\n

fun f(x: Any): String = if (x is Int) "int ${x + 1}" else if (x is Long) "long ${x * 3}" else if (x is Float) "float ${x / 2}" else if (x is Double) "double ${x + 0.5}" else if (x is String) "str ${x.length + 1}" else if (x is Char) "char ${x + 1}" else "other"\nfun g(x: Any?): Int = when (x) { is Int -> x * 2; is String -> x.length; else -> -1 }\nfun h(x: Any) = x is Int && x > 3\nfun k(x: Any) = x !is Int || x < 0\nfun main() {\n    for (v in listOf<Any>(1, 2L, 1.5f, 2.5, "ab", 'c', true)) println(f(v))\n    println(g(21)); println(g("abc")); println(g(null))\n    println(h(5)); println(h(1)); println(h("x")); println(k(5)); println(k(-1)); println(k("x"))\n    val v: Any = 3.5\n    println(when (v) { is Double -> v * 2; else -> 0.0 })\n    val w: Any = Int.MAX_VALUE\n    if (w is Int) println(w + 1)\n    val y = if (w is Int) w + 1 else 0\n    println(y)\n    when { v is Double -> println(v - 1) }\n    if (v !is Double) println("no") else println(v + 1)\n    val z: Any = 7L\n    if (z is Long && z > 6) println(z * 1000000000000L)\n}	int 2\nlong 6\nfloat 0.75\ndouble 3.0\nstr 3\nchar d\nother\n42\n3\n-1\ntrue\nfalse\nfalse\nfalse\ntrue\ntrue\n7.0\n-2147483648\n-2147483648\n2.5\n4.5\n7000000000000\n
